These flags are used in the AIS\+\_\+\+VESSEL.\+fields bitmask to indicate validity of data in the other message fields. 

When set, the data is valid. \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8h_a3fab820eda2bb267476cabfe5d07d62b}\label{common_8h_a3fab820eda2bb267476cabfe5d07d62b}} 
\index{common.h@{common.h}!HAVE\_ENUM\_AUTOTUNE\_AXIS@{HAVE\_ENUM\_AUTOTUNE\_AXIS}}
\index{HAVE\_ENUM\_AUTOTUNE\_AXIS@{HAVE\_ENUM\_AUTOTUNE\_AXIS}!common.h@{common.h}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define HAVE\+\_\+\+ENUM\+\_\+\+AUTOTUNE\+\_\+\+AXIS}



Axes that will be autotuned by MAV\+\_\+\+CMD\+\_\+\+DO\+\_\+\+AUTOTUNE\+\_\+\+ENABLE. 

Note that at least one flag must be set in MAV\+\_\+\+CMD\+\_\+\+DO\+\_\+\+AUTOTUNE\+\_\+\+ENABLE.\+param2\+: if none are set, the flight stack will tune its default set of axes. \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8h_ae2dff01e0f29f35cb83432e2b50cbcf2}\label{common_8h_ae2dff01e0f29f35cb83432e2b50cbcf2}}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{HAVE\_ENUM\_COMP\_METADATA\_TYPE}{HAVE\_ENUM\_COMP\_METADATA\_TYPE}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define HAVE\+\_\+\+ENUM\+\_\+\+COMP\+\_\+\+METADATA\+\_\+\+TYPE}



Supported component metadata types. 

These are used in the \char`\"{}general\char`\"{} metadata file returned by COMPONENT\+\_\+\+METADATA to provide information about supported metadata types. The types are not used directly in MAVLink messages. \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8h_ad1560f7efdcbdd34be45051d96099e01}\label{common_8h_ad1560f7efdcbdd34be45051d96099e01}} 
\index{common.h@{common.h}!HAVE\_ENUM\_FENCE\_TYPE@{HAVE\_ENUM\_FENCE\_TYPE}}
\index{HAVE\_ENUM\_FENCE\_TYPE@{HAVE\_ENUM\_FENCE\_TYPE}!common.h@{common.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{HAVE\_ENUM\_FENCE\_TYPE}{HAVE\_ENUM\_FENCE\_TYPE}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define HAVE\+\_\+\+ENUM\+\_\+\+FENCE\+\_\+\+TYPE}



Fence types to enable or disable when using MAV\+\_\+\+CMD\+\_\+\+DO\+\_\+\+FENCE\+\_\+\+ENABLE. 

Note that at least one of these flags must be set in MAV\+\_\+\+CMD\+\_\+\+DO\+\_\+\+FENCE\+\_\+\+ENABLE.\+param2. If none are set, the flight stack will ignore the field and enable/disable its default set of fences (usually all of them). \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8h_a54c20dfed88ca5f2354396304eee0b22}\label{common_8h_a54c20dfed88ca5f2354396304eee0b22}} 
\index{common.h@{common.h}!HAVE\_ENUM\_GIMBAL\_MANAGER\_CAP\_FLAGS@{HAVE\_ENUM\_GIMBAL\_MANAGER\_CAP\_FLAGS}}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{HAVE\_ENUM\_GIMBAL\_MANAGER\_CAP\_FLAGS}{HAVE\_ENUM\_GIMBAL\_MANAGER\_CAP\_FLAGS}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define HAVE\+\_\+\+ENUM\+\_\+\+GIMBAL\+\_\+\+MANAGER\+\_\+\+CAP\+\_\+\+FLAGS}



Gimbal manager high level capability flags (bitmap). 

The first 16 bits are identical to the GIMBAL\+\_\+\+DEVICE\+\_\+\+CAP\+\_\+\+FLAGS. However, the gimbal manager does not need to copy the flags from the gimbal but can also enhance the capabilities and thus add flags. \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8h_a3d53f2e42a54193892401b4835694780}\label{common_8h_a3d53f2e42a54193892401b4835694780}} 
\index{common.h@{common.h}!HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_BATTERY\_FAULT@{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_BATTERY\_FAULT}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define HAVE\+\_\+\+ENUM\+\_\+\+MAV\+\_\+\+BATTERY\+\_\+\+FAULT}



Smart battery supply status/fault flags (bitmask) for health indication. 

The battery must also report either MAV\+\_\+\+BATTERY\+\_\+\+CHARGE\+\_\+\+STATE\+\_\+\+FAILED or MAV\+\_\+\+BATTERY\+\_\+\+CHARGE\+\_\+\+STATE\+\_\+\+UNHEALTHY if any of these are set. \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8h_aa739c0baf76827b3ff4fea976239fef1}\label{common_8h_aa739c0baf76827b3ff4fea976239fef1}} 
\index{common.h@{common.h}!HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_BATTERY\_MODE@{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_BATTERY\_MODE}}
\index{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_BATTERY\_MODE@{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_BATTERY\_MODE}!common.h@{common.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_BATTERY\_MODE}{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_BATTERY\_MODE}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define HAVE\+\_\+\+ENUM\+\_\+\+MAV\+\_\+\+BATTERY\+\_\+\+MODE}



Battery mode. 

Note, the normal operation mode (i.\+e. when flying) should be reported as MAV\+\_\+\+BATTERY\+\_\+\+MODE\+\_\+\+UNKNOWN to allow message trimming in normal flight. \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8h_a073ec1e277b3d058a124e241c473d5ee}\label{common_8h_a073ec1e277b3d058a124e241c473d5ee}} 
\index{common.h@{common.h}!HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_CMD@{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_CMD}}
\index{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_CMD@{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_CMD}!common.h@{common.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_CMD}{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_CMD}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define HAVE\+\_\+\+ENUM\+\_\+\+MAV\+\_\+\+CMD}



Commands to be executed by the MAV. 

They can be executed on user request, or as part of a mission script. If the action is used in a mission, the parameter mapping to the waypoint/mission message is as follows\+: Param 1, Param 2, Param 3, Param 4, X\+: Param 5, Y\+:Param 6, Z\+:Param 7. This command list is similar what ARINC 424 is for commercial aircraft\+: A data format how to interpret waypoint/mission data. NaN and INT32\+\_\+\+MAX may be used in float/integer params (respectively) to indicate optional/default values (e.\+g. to use the component\textquotesingle{}s current yaw or latitude rather than a specific value). See \href{https://mavlink.io/en/guide/xml_schema.html\#MAV_CMD}{\texttt{ https\+://mavlink.\+io/en/guide/xml\+\_\+schema.\+html\#\+MAV\+\_\+\+CMD}} for information about the structure of the MAV\+\_\+\+CMD entries \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8h_a0b14132efff94f45db406fcd21223a6b}\label{common_8h_a0b14132efff94f45db406fcd21223a6b}} 
\index{common.h@{common.h}!HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_DATA\_STREAM@{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_DATA\_STREAM}}
\index{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_DATA\_STREAM@{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_DATA\_STREAM}!common.h@{common.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_DATA\_STREAM}{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_DATA\_STREAM}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define HAVE\+\_\+\+ENUM\+\_\+\+MAV\+\_\+\+DATA\+\_\+\+STREAM}



A data stream is not a fixed set of messages, but rather a recommendation to the autopilot software. 

Individual autopilots may or may not obey the recommended messages. \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8h_aa166bc8416acc84a1fc82fe163ca7580}\label{common_8h_aa166bc8416acc84a1fc82fe163ca7580}} 
\index{common.h@{common.h}!HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_FRAME@{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_FRAME}}
\index{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_FRAME@{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_FRAME}!common.h@{common.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_FRAME}{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_FRAME}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define HAVE\+\_\+\+ENUM\+\_\+\+MAV\+\_\+\+FRAME}



Coordinate frames used by MAVLink. 

Not all frames are supported by all commands, messages, or vehicles.

Global frames use the following naming conventions\+:
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item \char`\"{}\+GLOBAL\char`\"{}\+: Global coordinate frame with WGS84 latitude/longitude and altitude positive over mean sea level (MSL) by default. The following modifiers may be used with \char`\"{}\+GLOBAL\char`\"{}\+:
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item \char`\"{}\+RELATIVE\+\_\+\+ALT\char`\"{}\+: Altitude is relative to the vehicle home position rather than MSL.
\item \char`\"{}\+TERRAIN\+\_\+\+ALT\char`\"{}\+: Altitude is relative to ground level rather than MSL.
\item \char`\"{}\+INT\char`\"{}\+: Latitude/longitude (in degrees) are scaled by multiplying by 1E7.
\end{DoxyItemize}
\end{DoxyItemize}

Local frames use the following naming conventions\+:
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item \char`\"{}\+LOCAL\char`\"{}\+: Origin of local frame is fixed relative to earth. Unless otherwise specified this origin is the origin of the vehicle position-\/estimator (\char`\"{}\+EKF\char`\"{}).
\item \char`\"{}\+BODY\char`\"{}\+: Origin of local frame travels with the vehicle. NOTE, \char`\"{}\+BODY\char`\"{} does NOT indicate alignment of frame axis with vehicle attitude.
\item \char`\"{}\+OFFSET\char`\"{}\+: Deprecated synonym for \char`\"{}\+BODY\char`\"{} (origin travels with the vehicle). Not to be used for new frames.
\end{DoxyItemize}

Some deprecated frames do not follow these conventions (e.\+g. MAV\+\_\+\+FRAME\+\_\+\+BODY\+\_\+\+NED and MAV\+\_\+\+FRAME\+\_\+\+BODY\+\_\+\+OFFSET\+\_\+\+NED). \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8h_a3117c2b4e640828484c3a533ed808844}\label{common_8h_a3117c2b4e640828484c3a533ed808844}} 
\index{common.h@{common.h}!HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_FTP\_ERR@{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_FTP\_ERR}}
\index{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_FTP\_ERR@{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_FTP\_ERR}!common.h@{common.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_FTP\_ERR}{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_FTP\_ERR}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define HAVE\+\_\+\+ENUM\+\_\+\+MAV\+\_\+\+FTP\+\_\+\+ERR}



MAV FTP error codes (may be used in FILE\+\_\+\+TRANSFER\+\_\+\+PROTOCOL). 

See \href{https://mavlink.io/en/services/ftp.html}{\texttt{ https\+://mavlink.\+io/en/services/ftp.\+html}}. \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8h_ad1814693fca2ad42d6fe2db9598b0f8f}\label{common_8h_ad1814693fca2ad42d6fe2db9598b0f8f}}

\index{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_FTP\_OPCODE@{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_FTP\_OPCODE}!common.h@{common.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_FTP\_OPCODE}{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_FTP\_OPCODE}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define HAVE\+\_\+\+ENUM\+\_\+\+MAV\+\_\+\+FTP\+\_\+\+OPCODE}



MAV FTP opcodes (may be used in FILE\+\_\+\+TRANSFER\+\_\+\+PROTOCOL). 

See \href{https://mavlink.io/en/services/ftp.html}{\texttt{ https\+://mavlink.\+io/en/services/ftp.\+html}}. \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8h_ab0f9a9786e5a9c55c22afad6b242a886}\label{common_8h_ab0f9a9786e5a9c55c22afad6b242a886}} 
\index{common.h@{common.h}!HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_FUEL\_TYPE@{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_FUEL\_TYPE}}
\index{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_FUEL\_TYPE@{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_FUEL\_TYPE}!common.h@{common.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_FUEL\_TYPE}{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_FUEL\_TYPE}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define HAVE\+\_\+\+ENUM\+\_\+\+MAV\+\_\+\+FUEL\+\_\+\+TYPE}



Fuel types for use in FUEL\+\_\+\+TYPE. 

Fuel types specify the units for the maximum, available and consumed fuel, and for the flow rates. \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8h_a6d73f70cdb8de3a99789d101a61465eb}\label{common_8h_a6d73f70cdb8de3a99789d101a61465eb}} 
\index{common.h@{common.h}!HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_GENERATOR\_STATUS\_FLAG@{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_GENERATOR\_STATUS\_FLAG}}
\index{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_GENERATOR\_STATUS\_FLAG@{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_GENERATOR\_STATUS\_FLAG}!common.h@{common.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_GENERATOR\_STATUS\_FLAG}{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_GENERATOR\_STATUS\_FLAG}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define HAVE\+\_\+\+ENUM\+\_\+\+MAV\+\_\+\+GENERATOR\+\_\+\+STATUS\+\_\+\+FLAG}



Flags to report status/failure cases for a power generator (used in GENERATOR\+\_\+\+STATUS). 

Note that FAULTS are conditions that cause the generator to fail. Warnings are conditions that require attention before the next use (they indicate the system is not operating properly). \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8h_acde65e5e929e58909a2c868e812cea55}\label{common_8h_acde65e5e929e58909a2c868e812cea55}} 
\index{common.h@{common.h}!HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_MODE@{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_MODE}}
\index{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_MODE@{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_MODE}!common.h@{common.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_MODE}{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_MODE}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define HAVE\+\_\+\+ENUM\+\_\+\+MAV\+\_\+\+MODE}



Predefined OR-\/combined MAV\+\_\+\+MODE\+\_\+\+FLAG values. 

These can simplify using the flags when setting modes. Note that manual input is enabled in all modes as a safety override. \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8h_ae825aaf3506135b5ec43f30ecad8f328}\label{common_8h_ae825aaf3506135b5ec43f30ecad8f328}} 
\index{common.h@{common.h}!HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_MOUNT\_MODE@{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_MOUNT\_MODE}}
\index{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_MOUNT\_MODE@{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_MOUNT\_MODE}!common.h@{common.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_MOUNT\_MODE}{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_MOUNT\_MODE}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define HAVE\+\_\+\+ENUM\+\_\+\+MAV\+\_\+\+MOUNT\+\_\+\+MODE}



Enumeration of possible mount operation modes. 

This message is used by obsolete/deprecated gimbal messages. \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8h_a40e1da2c38e4848377aff8b25c7fa95d}\label{common_8h_a40e1da2c38e4848377aff8b25c7fa95d}} 
\index{common.h@{common.h}!HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_ROI@{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_ROI}}
\index{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_ROI@{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_ROI}!common.h@{common.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_ROI}{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_ROI}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define HAVE\+\_\+\+ENUM\+\_\+\+MAV\+\_\+\+ROI}



The ROI (region of interest) for the vehicle. 

This can be be used by the vehicle for camera/vehicle attitude alignment (see MAV\+\_\+\+CMD\+\_\+\+NAV\+\_\+\+ROI). \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8h_a9d65220b8a1b9b640e2017a619749241}\label{common_8h_a9d65220b8a1b9b640e2017a619749241}} 
\index{common.h@{common.h}!HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_SEVERITY@{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_SEVERITY}}
\index{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_SEVERITY@{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_SEVERITY}!common.h@{common.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_SEVERITY}{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_SEVERITY}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define HAVE\+\_\+\+ENUM\+\_\+\+MAV\+\_\+\+SEVERITY}



Indicates the severity level, generally used for status messages to indicate their relative urgency. 

Based on RFC-\/5424 using expanded definitions at\+: \href{http://www.kiwisyslog.com/kb/info:-syslog-message-levels/}{\texttt{ http\+://www.\+kiwisyslog.\+com/kb/info\+:-\/syslog-\/message-\/levels/}}. \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8h_a8c60a22f6a64e2a98bbf837a89578527}\label{common_8h_a8c60a22f6a64e2a98bbf837a89578527}} 
\index{common.h@{common.h}!HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_STANDARD\_MODE@{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_STANDARD\_MODE}}
\index{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_STANDARD\_MODE@{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_STANDARD\_MODE}!common.h@{common.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_STANDARD\_MODE}{HAVE\_ENUM\_MAV\_STANDARD\_MODE}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define HAVE\+\_\+\+ENUM\+\_\+\+MAV\+\_\+\+STANDARD\+\_\+\+MODE}



Standard modes with a well understood meaning across flight stacks and vehicle types. 

For example, most flight stack have the concept of a \char`\"{}return\char`\"{} or \char`\"{}\+RTL\char`\"{} mode that takes a vehicle to safety, even though the precise mechanics of this mode may differ. The modes supported by a flight stack can be queried using AVAILABLE\+\_\+\+MODES and set using MAV\+\_\+\+CMD\+\_\+\+DO\+\_\+\+SET\+\_\+\+STANDARD\+\_\+\+MODE. The current mode is streamed in CURRENT\+\_\+\+MODE. See \href{https://mavlink.io/en/services/standard_modes.html}{\texttt{ https\+://mavlink.\+io/en/services/standard\+\_\+modes.\+html}} \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8h_abb7a36865187c03064b1d8592beb8d4e}\label{common_8h_abb7a36865187c03064b1d8592beb8d4e}} 
\index{common.h@{common.h}!HAVE\_ENUM\_MISSION\_STATE@{HAVE\_ENUM\_MISSION\_STATE}}
\index{HAVE\_ENUM\_MISSION\_STATE@{HAVE\_ENUM\_MISSION\_STATE}!common.h@{common.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{HAVE\_ENUM\_MISSION\_STATE}{HAVE\_ENUM\_MISSION\_STATE}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define HAVE\+\_\+\+ENUM\+\_\+\+MISSION\+\_\+\+STATE}



States of the mission state machine. 

Note that these states are independent of whether the mission is in a mode that can execute mission items or not (is suspended). They may not all be relevant on all vehicles. \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8h_ab194c8cb724fb8b804d5b49d7ddf3ef6}\label{common_8h_ab194c8cb724fb8b804d5b49d7ddf3ef6}}

\index{common.h@{common.h}!HAVE\_ENUM\_POSITION\_TARGET\_TYPEMASK@{HAVE\_ENUM\_POSITION\_TARGET\_TYPEMASK}}
\index{HAVE\_ENUM\_POSITION\_TARGET\_TYPEMASK@{HAVE\_ENUM\_POSITION\_TARGET\_TYPEMASK}!common.h@{common.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{HAVE\_ENUM\_POSITION\_TARGET\_TYPEMASK}{HAVE\_ENUM\_POSITION\_TARGET\_TYPEMASK}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define HAVE\+\_\+\+ENUM\+\_\+\+POSITION\+\_\+\+TARGET\+\_\+\+TYPEMASK}



Bitmap to indicate which dimensions should be ignored by the vehicle\+: a value of 0b0000000000000000 or 0b0000001000000000 indicates that none of the setpoint dimensions should be ignored. 

If bit 9 is set the floats afx afy afz should be interpreted as force instead of acceleration. \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8h_a67dc34f74ba9c49a83b1c0f7299dac07}\label{common_8h_a67dc34f74ba9c49a83b1c0f7299dac07}} 
\index{common.h@{common.h}!HAVE\_ENUM\_PREFLIGHT\_STORAGE\_MISSION\_ACTION@{HAVE\_ENUM\_PREFLIGHT\_STORAGE\_MISSION\_ACTION}}
\index{HAVE\_ENUM\_PREFLIGHT\_STORAGE\_MISSION\_ACTION@{HAVE\_ENUM\_PREFLIGHT\_STORAGE\_MISSION\_ACTION}!common.h@{common.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{HAVE\_ENUM\_PREFLIGHT\_STORAGE\_MISSION\_ACTION}{HAVE\_ENUM\_PREFLIGHT\_STORAGE\_MISSION\_ACTION}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define HAVE\+\_\+\+ENUM\+\_\+\+PREFLIGHT\+\_\+\+STORAGE\+\_\+\+MISSION\+\_\+\+ACTION}



Actions for reading and writing plan information (mission, rally points, geofence) between persistent and volatile storage when using MAV\+\_\+\+CMD\+\_\+\+PREFLIGHT\+\_\+\+STORAGE. 

(Commonly missions are loaded from persistent storage (flash/\+EEPROM) into volatile storage (RAM) on startup and written back when they are changed.) \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8h_a5a9b278ec9df43d17aeecc13b51a3f37}\label{common_8h_a5a9b278ec9df43d17aeecc13b51a3f37}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define HAVE\+\_\+\+ENUM\+\_\+\+PREFLIGHT\+\_\+\+STORAGE\+\_\+\+PARAMETER\+\_\+\+ACTION}



Actions for reading/writing parameters between persistent and volatile storage when using MAV\+\_\+\+CMD\+\_\+\+PREFLIGHT\+\_\+\+STORAGE. 

(Commonly parameters are loaded from persistent storage (flash/\+EEPROM) into volatile storage (RAM) on startup and written back when they are changed.) \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8h_afd97d570b8b254e27e42c0381c0ca2a2}\label{common_8h_afd97d570b8b254e27e42c0381c0ca2a2}}
